# Agents · D · part 16

857 entries, design-critic to design-refactor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **design-critic**: Independent visual critic for Maquettekit work. Give it a review pass folder (for example .maquettekit/review/pass-2) or screenshot paths, plus one sentence on what the product is and who it is for. It judges only what the screenshots show — without the code, the conversation or the builder's intentions — and returns rubric scores with evidence, the formulaic patterns it sees, and the three changes that would most improve the page. Use it after a page passes "maquettekit check" and before calling the work finished. It never edits files. ([kodiii/maquettekit](https://github.com/kodiii/maquettekit/blob/HEAD/plugins/maquettekit/agents/design-critic.md))
- **design-critic**: Read-only design critic. Reviews a design brief, mockup description, screenshot, or front-end code against its stated brief: hierarchy, clarity of the primary action, consistency with tokens, generic-look tells, and states. Use for a final check before handoff. ([Krosebrook/augmentor-packs](https://github.com/Krosebrook/augmentor-packs/blob/HEAD/plugins/designer-augmentor/agents/design-critic.md))
- **design-critic**: Renders a generated 3D model from multiple angles and critiques it VISION-GROUNDED against the packaged reference brief, the artistic style params, and any user reference images — fusing the validator's KERNEL METRICS (watertight/genus/components/wall-p05/overhang) with the renders — returning per-axis 0-100 scores (silhouette, proportion, feature presence, style adherence, print-readiness) and a ship/revise/escalate/reject verdict with specific fixes that cite the reference. Use inside the model3d / grill-me fidelity loop to judge whether a model matches the request before it is shipped, and… ([kryptobaseddev/3d-ai-studio](https://github.com/kryptobaseddev/3d-ai-studio/blob/HEAD/registry/agents/design-critic.md))
- **design-critic**: Senior design reviewer with fresh eyes. Use to critique a screen, flow or built UI that someone else designed. Receives the brief and the rendered design, never the designer's reasoning, and returns a severity-ranked critique with principles and fixes. ([kscarfi/ux-grandmaster](https://github.com/kscarfi/ux-grandmaster/blob/HEAD/agents/design-critic.md) · 2 copies)
- **design-critic**: Independent critique of a UI surface against the product personality, UI constitution, design tokens, and design-craft guide. Use after building or reshaping any screen, panel, toolbar, or dialog — complements the ui-polish skill with a fresh pair of eyes. Reports only, never edits (no Edit/Write tools; Bash is for inspection). ([kumeS/RUST-Tauri-claude-harness-kit](https://github.com/kumeS/RUST-Tauri-claude-harness-kit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-critic.md))
- **design-critic**: Design Critique Partner for smart home inventory app. Nielsen's heuristics, AI vision UX, inventory management flows, and mobile-first responsive review. Use for design reviews. ([kvadou/gatheredos](https://github.com/kvadou/gatheredos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-critic.md))
- **design-critic**: Independent architecture and implementation plan reviewer. Invoked by the project-design-gate skill during DESIGN DELIBERATION. Reads the proposed implementation plan and existing project files, identifies real technical defects, security risks, edge cases, failure modes, and better alternatives. Returns actionable findings with severity, evidence, proposed corrections, and blocking status. Does not change any source files. Read-only during design review. ([lbbaalaji0406/cap23-blood-donation](https://github.com/lbbaalaji0406/cap23-blood-donation/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/design-critic.md))
- **design-critic**: 설계 비판 에이전트. 설계 초안의 암묵적 가정을 도전하고, 불필요한 복잡성을 식별하며, 문제의 근본 원인이 올바르게 정의되었는지 검증한다. 설계 단계에서 선택적으로 호출되며, 설계 확정 전 사각지대를 찾는 역할이다. &lt;example&gt; User: 설계서 비판 검토 요청 Agent: [CHALLENGE] "모든 사용자"가 실제로 법인을 포함하는지 미검증, [SIMPLIFY] 캐시 레이어가 현재 트래픽 수준에서 불필요, [ROOT-CAUSE] 성능 문제의 원인이 쿼리가 아닌 인덱스 누락일 가능성 &lt;/example&gt; ([letsgojh0810/ticket-hub](https://github.com/letsgojh0810/ticket-hub/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-critic.md) · 7 copies)
- **design-critic**: Critiques screenshots of one screen against a design rubric and returns ranked findings, each citing a rubric item, a screenshot, a state and a viewport. - **design-critic**: Read-only. Use after UI changes to critique a page or component's hierarchy, typography, color, spacing and states. ([MonkeysCodeAI/monkeyscode-plugins](https://github.com/MonkeysCodeAI/monkeyscode-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/design-studio/agents/design-critic.md))
- **design-critic**: Ruthless anti-slop design critic. Looks at a RENDERED screen of the project (screenshot) and judges it against the design philosophy + the AI-slop signatures. Returns a SLOP / SHIP verdict with specific, cited failures, each graded Critical/Major/Minor/Nit, and exact fixes. Run on every new or redesigned screen BEFORE it ships. In picture mode it judges a PROPOSED picture against the project's kit reference before the operator sees it, and returns KIT: MATCH | KIT: DRIFT. It fights the statistical mean — it does not rubber-stamp. ([mrdombie/agent-harness](https://github.com/mrdombie/agent-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/design-critic.md))
- **design-critic**: Reviews UI work built by ui-designer for genericness, accessibility, and polish ([MuhRakhaP/Wargaja](https://github.com/MuhRakhaP/Wargaja/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/design-critic.md))
- **design-critic**: Fresh-context design review of UI screenshots against the brief and the AI-cliché blacklist. Use after the visual-review-loop produces screenshots, before declaring UI work done — the reviewer has NOT seen the code and judges only what is on screen, which is the point. ([muzzamilhassan/automation](https://github.com/muzzamilhassan/automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-critic.md))
- **design-critic**: Use this agent for visual / editorial critique rounds in this portfolio's act-critic iteration workflow. Locks the response to the four-dimension structure (實用 / 美觀 / 一致 / 創意) so critic prompts cannot drift back into "驗收 / 問題 / 建議" three-part shape. Invoke whenever the main agent has just made a UI/visual change and captured screenshots — this agent reads the PNGs cold and grades each change against the four dimensions. Does not implement, craft cards, or run issue mechanics; it facilitates the decision, then hands off. ([lalli-oni/cards](https://github.com/lalli-oni/cards/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/design-facilitator.md) · ★1)
- **design-facilitator**: Neutral convergence judge for the design-council skill. After each round it maps where the lenses agree and genuinely disagree, names the crux of each dispute, classifies convergence as evidence-resolved or capitulation, and decides another round / converged / deadlock. Read-only; owns the convergence verdict so the orchestrator never declares its own debate settled. ([SApplefeld/Plugins](https://github.com/SApplefeld/Plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/grimoire/agents/design-facilitator.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
